Finding Idea: There are some category of content: Skits, POV, Talking Head, and Dance; New category is Street Interview. You have to pick one of em. Anything that doesn't require talking, Skits and Dance, would simply get you extraordinary reach... to tier-3 countries. So, For doing AI OFM, POV, Talking Head and Street Interviews are gold.  Once you pick a category, next is to see some content of that category, interact with those content (Like, Save, Share, Comment), and keep seeing exact same video for multiple iteration to fix your profile Algo to show content around those reels. That's how you get idea.

Producing: Simple, if you're somewhat comfortable or low on budget, go for comfy to generate the content. I had generated 80 reels clip with $6.7; all of those were skits, under 5 sec; that's a thing. but if there were talking head or other that is around 20 sec, I could generate 40 reels with same cost. 
If you're comfortable with trial and erroring, no budget issue, not comfortable with tech stuff, simply use Wavespeed Desktop. Put the prompt, run the job, simple.
Wavespeed API can be used for batch content generation too. So you'll have to put all prompt, ref image, as much as you want, then do one action (run a single script or click a single button), all prompt will keep generating content one by one; while you can chill the f out, doing something else. Although that batching would require you to create a script on it.
